# The First Dawn Art Bible

Entries for the final cut of **First Dawn** (1.9 lane B, the art run): the
118 creatures, in the order of the cut list (rarity, then colour; see
"Catalog order" below), followed by the four set tokens. The 48 non-creature
cards are in [spell-art.md](../spell-art.md) under "First Dawn
non-creatures". The **First Dawn register** in [index.md](index.md) section
4d governs every palette, lighting pair, species tell, costume and
large-subject rule below, the First Dawn tokens, named cast, plain beasts and
places in section 7 are canon here, and the global style contract in section
2 remains in force above it. The entries were written in six colour batches
(green, red, white, blue, black, multicolour) on the pilot's shape, then
merged and validated against the cut; ten of them are the art pilot's own.

**Card facts are transcribed from the final cut** (the owner's full card
review of 2026-09-29, with the coverage proposals and rework drafts applied),
not from card data: `src/data/cards/first-dawn.ts` does not exist yet. When
it lands, `check-art-bible` becomes the authority and every fact line here
must agree with it. Holo follows the Drowned Deep convention (common none,
rare and super rare sheen, double super rare shiny, ultra rare prism). The
keyword segment uses the engine's keyword ids (`wardingGaze`, `firstBlade`,
`twinBlades`, `bloodoath` and the rest), the form `check-art-bible` compares
against `card.keywords`. Mechanics that are not engine keywords (Nine Lives,
Preserve, Whispers, Tithe, Rite, Skim, Empower, Hauntlink) and keywords a
card only gains on a condition (Pack-Runner Raptor's Warcry) stay off the
fact line, as in `drowned-deep.md`; the entries' prose still draws them.

**How the pipeline reads this file.** `gen-card-art.ts` knows the
`first-dawn` faction: `--faction first-dawn` runs every entry here, tokens
included, and `--only <ids>` a subset. Without `--out-dir` the WebPs land in
`public/assets/art/cards/<id>.webp` and `gen-art-manifest` reruns, as the
Drowned Deep art did before its data landed; with `--out-dir <scratch>` they
land outside `public/` and the manifest is not touched, which is the route
for a review batch. `gen-spell-art.ts` reads the 48 non-creature prompts from
`spell-art.md`, whose roster check now carries their ids. Every other entry here
gets `gen-card-art.ts`'s figure preamble, not the waist-up portrait preamble
the older sets use: the top of her head a third of the way down the canvas and
her knees no lower than three quarters down, the story beside her between her
head and her knees, and a
crop that keeps the full raw width with her head top on the y 179 line
(calibration round 1, 2026-09-29; docs/art-pipeline.md). Entries that open
their Prompt with "NO woman" (the beast-alone cards and all four tokens) get
its beast preamble instead. `gen-spell-art.ts` has no such switch and needs
none, since its preamble names no figure.

Five register rules bind hardest and are repeated here because a generator
breaks them first:

- **Every head top at or below y ≈ 179, human or dinosaur**, with horns,
  frills and crests counted (section 3's headroom rule on the 216 window,
  y 138 to 662), **and nothing climbs above that line**: tail tips, skulls,
  raised weapons and wings stay under it too (index.md section 4d, the
  pilot's lesson).
- **Story beside her, never below her knees** (set-wide, 2026-09-29): every
  secondary story element (a nest, an egg, bones, a paddle tip, a tail's tip,
  a Hatchling, a dropped weapon) sits beside her at hip or waist height, never
  lower than her knees, and nothing sits between her and the viewer below her
  waist: never at her feet, in the lower foreground or on the ground before
  her. Where it needs the ground, raise the ground beside her (a fern bank, a
  boulder or a ledge at hip or waist height). The card window ends at 82.7% of
  the frame (y 662), and anything lower is cropped; calibration round 2 showed
  that an element placed in front of her lands low even when the prompt names
  its height, while one placed beside her lands where asked.
- **Scale by distance, never by shrinking the woman**; mount and rider never
  fuse; every tail states its count, root and tip.
- **The costume coverage rule**: chest and hips fully covered, visible ties,
  one more named layer, never string-minimal or slipping.
- **NO-TEXT, the stone-age variant**: cave paintings show animals and hunts
  only, no pictograph rows, no tally marks, no carved symbols.

**The Mark bead stays cyan** (owner ruling P12, 2026-09-28), as in Starborne
and Drowned Deep: a Mark is the section 4d bead, one small bead of biolume
cyan `#5ff0e0` in the skin or hide, one per Mark, countable, at mid-height.
Every Prompt carries exactly one cyan form: the counted beads on a named
bearer, or "no cyan glow, no cyan light".

- **Where it appears:** on the bearer, when an entry's art shows a creature
  carrying a Mark as a state (the creature a Mark card has marked, drawn
  close enough for a bead to read), with the stated count and section 4b's
  placement clause. That entry drops "no cyan glow, no cyan light" for "cyan
  only in the [N] beads on [the bearer], nowhere else in the frame". The two
  Nine Lives cards (Sabrekin Prowler, Ash-Cat Ambusher) draw the returned
  creature's mark as one bead on the upper arm; a Mark card that marks
  another creature may put the bead on the woman who carries it (Herd-Guardian
  Longneck on her own arm, Egg of the First Dawn on the tending woman), and
  the white heat ordeals draw the bead as the only trace of the test (owner,
  2026-09-29: all accepted).
- **Where it does not:** the sky, the Ice Wall, water, fire, eyes, body paint
  and cave paintings (section 4d); any token's canon art, since a Mark is a
  game state a token may or may not carry; and a card whose Mark lands on
  something the frame shows only at a distance (Great-Horn Herder's
  sauropod), where a bead would not read. The blue batch, where ice and sea
  water invite cyan, adds "no cyan anywhere in the frame" and names every ice
  surface glacier white-blue and every water surface deep blue and slate.

## The clans and their frames

- **Green, the Fern clan and the herds.** Leaf-dyed hide and woven grass,
  the Long Grass and the fern forest. Green carries eight Dinokin Hornbacks;
  each is fixed apart by age, build, skin, hair, pose and prop in its entry
  (Vessa, Herd-Caller, Old Bull, Tusk-and-Claw, Horn-Crest Charger,
  Moss-Hide, Horn-Bearer, Grazing), so no two drift into one face.
- **Red, the Ember clan.** Fire-walkers, war-painters and raptor packs in
  scorched leather with ochre and ash bands of body paint, flint at the belt.
  Fire is only ever a prop or a small practical under the one low-sun key.
  Red carries the set's heaviest load of Provoked (the blow survived, the
  striker drawn as a broken spear, a burst stone, a spent coal or a
  recoiling beast, never a wound) and of Hunt. Every plain raptor is the
  Pack Raptor design.
- **White, the Hearth clan and its herd.** Fire-keepers, elders and the
  shepherdesses of the long-necks, in bleached pale hide with bone and shell
  beads; high sandstone cave mouths, ring hearths, reed beds, the herd's
  fords. White's art is block-shaped: walls that hold, a heartbeat after the
  blow, and heat ordeals that test the clan without a mark on the skin (no
  burns, no scars), whose only trace is a Mark's bead. Plated armour on its
  Longnecks and Armourbacks is worn bone armour, clothing, not a species
  tell.
- **Blue, the Sky and Ice clans.** Pterosaur riders of the Cliff Nests and
  frost-seers of the Ice Wall, in wind-cut pale leathers and glacier-white
  furs, invented, never Arctic peoples' dress: every prompt with a woman
  says so, and every one set in snow, ice or sea wind adds "no parkas".
  Skyborne means airborne: the two Skyborne Human Scouts ride the Sky clan's
  riding pterosaur (section 7). The fossil line in blue is bones in ice and
  riverbed, one bone drawn per card milled.
- **Black, the Tar clan.** Bone-callers, ash-witches and spearwomen of the
  Tar Flats in tar-glossed dark-brown hide with ash-grey paint in bands,
  obsidian blades glossy dark grey with the sky in them, beast teeth, amber
  beads and clean fossil bone. Patience and appetite, not decay: the tar
  keeps what falls in and gives it back as clean stone-coloured bone. No
  rot, no human bones, no witch hats or cauldrons, no glowing magic; tar is
  glossy dark brown `#3d2a1a` / `#8a6a48`, never black, and Tar-Bones is
  drawn exactly to its section 7 canon, skull low, never reared.
- **Multicolour, in the gold frame.** The palette leans on the gold anchor
  (`#e8c95a` / `#7a5a18`, accent `#fff2b8`) with each colour carried by
  costume and scene: B/G the Hunt and the fossils, U/B the fossil line, R/G
  the core charge, G/W the herd, W/U the fliers and taps, R/W the warband.

The plain beasts (the plain tyrant, horned beast, sauropod, raptor,
pterosaurs, armoured beast, woolly mammoth and woolly rhinoceros) are drawn
from one design each, fixed in index.md section 7, so the valley stays one
world across colours.

## From the pilot to the full set

The art pilot (1.9 lane B, wave 2, 2026-09-28) drew ten hard cases from
section 11 of the approved identity brief
([first-dawn-brief.md](../expansions/drafts/first-dawn-brief.md)), eleven
images because case 7 is two, plus the two summit boss portraits (rungs 27
and 28), from entries written against the overplan
([first-dawn-overplan.md](../expansions/drafts/first-dawn-overplan.md)) before
the cut. Large non-humanoid subjects were new territory for the prompt
recipe, so those images went through the owner's eyes before the full run.

| # | Entry | Id | Hard case and its named control | At the cut |
| ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Kesh, Raptor-Rider | `fd-kesh-raptor-rider` | a raptor rider at a sprint: no fused bodies, tail root and tip | kept |
| 2 | Great-Horn Herder | `fd-great-horn-herder` | a shepherdess beside a sauropod: scale by distance, head inside the band | redone |
| 3 | Korru, Eldest of the Trackers | `fd-korru-eldest-tracker` | a hunter facing a tyrant: two creatures, count control, no gore | kept |
| 4 | Frill-Neck Stalker | `fd-frill-neck-stalker` | a horned Dinokin warrior: the frill against the headroom rule | cut |
| 5 | Scorch-Tail Raptor | `fd-scorch-tail` | a raptor Dinokin: tail root and tip | redone |
| 6 | Asha and Shree, Sky-Riders | `fd-sky-riders-pact` | a pterosaur rider airborne: no fused bodies, count control | kept |
| 7a | The Horned Herd | `fd-horned-herd` | an adult horned beast alone: no woman in frame, head inside the band | kept |
| 7b | Hatchling | `tok-hatchling` | the same beast newborn: visibly young, no human child | kept (token) |
| 8 | Tar-Bones | `tok-tar-bones` | a fossil rising from the tar: clean stone-coloured bone, no gore | kept (token) |
| 9 | The Painted Cave | `fd-painted-cave` | cave art: animals and hunts only, no text, no tally marks | cut |
| 10 | Pack-Caller of the Red Cliffs | `fd-pack-caller` | exactly one raptor at her side, the one the card makes: count control | redone |
| R27 | Tahla, Shepherdess of Thunder | `fd-tahla-shepherdess` | the rung 27 tower portrait: count control, scale by distance | kept |
| R28 | Oru, the Tyrant Queen | `fd-oru-tyrant-queen` | the rung 28 tower portrait: the costume rule, tail root and tip | kept |

**The pilot's verdict** (owner rulings P10-P15, 2026-09-28). The recipe
reads as First Dawn (P11: "Mostly, yes. I think it's a good look overall.").
The thirteen pilot images are kept locally and gitignored, in the main
checkout's research folder (`research/first-dawn-art-pilot/`), with the
contact sheet `pilot-sheet.png` and the assembled prompts; their retained
raws are in `%TEMP%/gen-card-art/`. They are **candidates for the art run,
not shipped art**: each is re-checked against its entry below. (The sheet's
other two images, Swan-Lake Sovereign and Brood Communion, were approved to
replace the shipped art, P10, and are committed.) Three are redone from the
tightened entries below, and one is re-cropped:

- **Tar-Bones (re-cropped, no regeneration):** the pilot crop put the skull
  above the card window (its top at about y 48), because the person detector
  boxed the tail of bones. Cropping the retained raw from its top row
  (offset -239) puts the skull top at about y 197, inside the window, with
  the tar still in band. The candidate in the research folder is that
  re-crop; the tightened entry below stands if it is ever regenerated.
- **Scorch-Tail Raptor:** the tail tip rose above the card window (about
  y 70); the redo keeps the tail in one low shallow curve.
- **Pack-Caller of the Red Cliffs:** the pilot drew three raptors as a count
  test; the card makes one Pack Raptor, so the art shows one (P15: the art
  shows what the card makes).
- **Great-Horn Herder** (owner, 2026-09-28: redo): the sauropod's head
  (about y 100), the two pterosaurs and the raised crook all rose above the
  card window. The redo drops the pterosaurs, lays the crook level and reads
  "Great-Horn" as her calling horn; her Duty's Mark stays undrawn, since at
  scale by distance a bead on the sauropod would not read.

**Case 2 lives on a woman's card** (confirmed, P13): the brief's case (a
shepherdess beside a sauropod, typed after the woman, D2) is Great-Horn
Herder, a Human Shepherd whose Duty marks a creature with Attack 4 or more.
**The Walking Mountain is beast-only** (P13): its Prompt opens "NO woman", it
reads huge by distance, its whole body and raised head sit inside the band,
and its Provoked Hatchlings are foreshadowed as exactly two hatchlings of the
one Hatchling design, moved to the near bank so they read at scale.

**Cut at the final cut:** Frill-Neck Stalker and The Painted Cave are not in
the final cut, so their pilot entries are gone from this file (git history
keeps them). The Painted Cave's lesson stays in index.md section 4d: cave
paintings show animals and hunts only.

**Added after the pilot: Vyra, Ember-Sky Rider** (the owner's ninth Ultra
Rare, 2026-09-29). The pilot draft carried the working id
`fd-vyra-ember-sky-rider`; the cut's id is `fd-vyra-ember-sky`, and her entry
uses it. She is the set's sole red Skyborne card, a red rider on a pterosaur
(the owner's words), framed in the session as a pie exception since the
brief gives the sky to W/U; she is its Shivan Dragon: an Ember-clan woman on
a plain red and ochre pterosaur, a Human Rider (D2) with no species tells.
Her entry follows the section 4d recipe and the pilot's lessons, taking its
shape from Asha and Shree (the airborne rider, never fused) and Kesh (the
Ember-clan rider, the levelled spear). **Its Prompt is a DRAFT: Fable
reviews it before any image is generated.**

## Owner rulings at assembly (2026-09-29)

The authors' flags were ruled together; everything below is accepted as the
entries draw it.

- **Wild Raptors shows exactly one raptor** (P15): the card is one creature,
  and the plural name is not a count.
- **Species tells, three each** (beastkin.md): Mammothkin draws ears, tusks
  and wool mane, no tail; Woolly Rhinokin ears, two nose-horns and wool coat,
  no tail; Sabretooth ears, sabre fangs and bobbed tail. The **Swimmer**
  tells (paddle tail, fin ridges, wet sheen) are ratified (index.md
  section 4d).
- **Name readings:** Great-Horn Herder's "Great-Horn" is her calling horn;
  the Ember-Crest and Blaze-Crest Tyrants' crest is a swept-back hairstyle,
  not a crest; Rage-Horn Tyrant's horn is a bone war-horn at her hip, with no
  horns on her head; Scar-Giver Elder and Scar-Singer of the Hearth draw the
  Mark bead as the "scar", and nobody carries scars; Scar-Proud Initiate
  carries one old healed scar for the name, not a Provoked tell; Old Bull of
  the Herd's "Bull" is a herd title; Tusk-and-Claw Hornback hunts a plain
  woolly mammoth (the tusk) with flint claw-knives (the claw); Carved Tusk is
  a tusk blade bound with hide, with no spirit cords.
- **Scenes:** Swallowed by the Tar is a raptor sinking calm and whole;
  Tar-Choke's victim is an Ember-clan hunter, never bound; The Elders'
  Verdict draws Sever as the beast driven out of the valley, with no magic;
  Longneck Calf-Guard draws no calf, since the card makes none; the two
  Skyborne Human Scouts (Cliff-Top Scout, Egg-Snatcher) ride the Sky clan's
  plain riding pterosaur, with no wings of their own.
- **Token ids** here use `tok-*`, the house precedent; the overplan spells
  them `fd-tok-*`, and transcription picks one.

## Catalog order

The 118 creature entries follow the cut list in rarity order (Ultra Rare, Double Super Rare, Super Rare, Rare, Common), then colour (green, red, white, blue, black, multicolour), keeping the cut list's order inside each group. That is the order the card data file takes when it lands; the non-creatures follow the same rule in `spell-art.md`.

### Vessa, the Great Horn — `fd-vessa-great-horn`
- **Card facts:** {3}{G}{G} · G · 5/6 · wardingGaze · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Vessa, the Fern clan's Great Horn and green's Provoked flagship: strike her and the whole herd is marked, and the marked herd tramples. A Dinokin Hornback with the three Hornback tells: exactly two brow horns curving forward (the longest in the valley, bone ivory banded with sandstone), a bony frill fanning back behind her head, one thick blunt-tipped tail. Small scaled patches at her temples and shoulders are the Dinokin skin, not a tell. Forties, very tall and heavy-shouldered, deep umber skin, long silver-white hair in one thick low braid.
- **Personality / mood:** The matriarch who takes the blow meant for the herd and hands the herd her temper. Immovable, protective, slow to anger and slower to stop.
- **Pose & composition:** Front three-quarter, knees up, centred on a trampled herd crossing, weight already coming forward, dust and a few chipped scale flakes still falling from her left shoulder where the blow landed. Her right arm swept back and open toward the herd. Exactly two adult plain horned beasts stand in the mid-ground at the left and right thirds, a few paces behind her, heads lowered and lower than hers, each carrying exactly one cyan bead on its shoulder (y 380 to 420). She carries none: her Provoked marks each *other* creature. Frill top is her head top and the highest point in frame, at or below y 179. All three tells in the window: horns and frill at the head, the tail curling around her left hip, root and blunt tip between her waist and mid-thigh.
- **Costume & attire:** Leaf-dyed hide two-piece, chest and hips covered, rawhide ties, a long cloak of woven grass and fern-green hide over one shoulder, bone-and-amber armlets.
- **Palette:** G `#4fa06a` / `#123a22`, accent `#a9dcae`; fern green `#4f8a3c` / `#8cc063`; bone ivory `#efe6d0` horns; sandstone `#d9b98c` dust; cyan `#5ff0e0` only in the two beads.
- **Lighting:** Low sun from the left, dawn-peach key raking across her face and the falling dust; sky-blue `#8cc4ec` rim on her right side and along the beasts' backs.
- **Expression:** A level, heavy-lidded stare at the viewer, the one who struck her; jaw set, a warning rather than a snarl.
- **Props / weapon:** None in hand: the horns are the weapon. The two marked beasts are the secondary canon element.
- **Background:** The Long Grass at the herd crossing: trampled lanes, a warm river steaming, the ring of smoking mountains, the dawn sky bright and low behind her shoulders.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the bright dawn sky behind her shoulders is the centred glow; keep her face and the two beads smooth so the rings do not muddy them.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the whole card in one frame, the matriarch struck and unmoved, the herd marked and ready to run.
- **Prompt:** EXACTLY ONE adult woman and EXACTLY TWO adult plain horned dinosaurs, nothing else alive in the frame, the woman a dinosaur monster-girl with exactly three species features and no others: exactly two long bone-ivory brow horns banded with sandstone, curving forward from above her eyebrows and pointing forward, never upward, a bony frill fanning back behind her head no taller than a hand's width above her crown, and exactly one thick scaled tail emerging from the base of her spine at the tailbone, never from her hip, side or waist, curling around her left hip and ending in exactly one blunt tip, root and blunt tip both between her waist and mid-thigh, inside the middle band, very tall and heavy-shouldered, in her forties, deep umber skin with small scaled patches at her temples and shoulders as part of her skin and not a species feature, a narrow row of small rounded scales from her nape down her spine and along the top of her tail, the Dinokin skin, not a species ### Korru, Eldest of the Trackers — `fd-korru-eldest-tracker`
- **Card facts:** {3}{G}{G} · G · 4/5 · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Korru, the Fern clan's eldest tracker, late fifties: lean and hard, deep brown weathered skin, grey-streaked black hair in one low braid. Her Duty is a Hunt: case 3, a hunter facing a tyrant, both whole.
- **Personality / mood:** "Eldest of the Trackers": patient, unhurried, still here.
- **Pose & composition:** Korru at the left third, three-quarter from behind, turning her face toward the tyrant, spear braced low. The tyrant at the right, ten paces off across open ground, head lowered to her eye level, jaws closed. Her head top at or below y 179; its head y 320. The instant before contact.

- **Lighting:** Low sun behind the tyrant, dawn-peach key, its shadow reaching toward her; sky-blue `#8cc4ec` rim.
- **Expression:** Calm, narrowed eyes.
- **Props / weapon:** Long flint spear; the plain tyrant.
- **Background:** A trampled fern clearing, steam, the volcanic ring.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the sun behind the tyrant is the centred glow.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the whole Hunt in one frame.

### Ashka, Who Walks in Fire — `fd-ashka-fire-walker`
- **Card facts:** {2}{R}{R} · R · 3/3 · warcry · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Ashka, the Ember clan's fire-walker: a legendary Human Firekeeper, so no species tells. Late thirties, tall, long-limbed and sinewy, dark copper-brown skin, long black hair with one ash-grey streak bound low at the nape, ochre cheek stripes and ash-white forearm bands. Red's self-damage showcase: her Duty pings any creature, herself included, and Provoked sends 2 at the opponent. Her secondary canon element is the ember horn, a hollow aurochs horn that carries the clan's live coals.
- **Personality / mood:** "Who Walks in Fire": the fire strikes her and she enjoys it. Warcry: she never waits.
- **Pose & composition:** Front three-quarter, seen from a few steps back, head to shins, striding barefoot toward the viewer down a trench of glowing coals running back from the lower centre. A gout of sparks has just burst against her left side and is still falling, never higher than her shoulders; her right arm swings out low, flicking one glowing coal toward a target past the right edge (hip height). Left hand on the ember horn. Head top at or below y 179, the highest point in frame; flames never above her knees.
- **Costume & attire:** Scorched-leather two-piece, chest and hips covered, sinew ties; a long hide wrap skirt slit at one thigh; a dark red-brown shoulder pelt; amber beads; bare feet.
- **Palette:** R `#d95436` / `#5e0f0f`, accent `#f7b267`; lava orange `#ff7a2e` / `#ffb057` coals; bone ivory `#efe6d0` horn; basalt grey `#5b6166` terrace.
- **Lighting:** Low sun on the horizon just left of centre behind her shoulder, dawn-peach key raking from the left; sky-blue `#8cc4ec` rim on her right edges. The coals are a bright prop, never the key.
- **Expression:** A fierce, delighted grin, teeth bared, eyes locked on her target.
- **Props / weapon:** The ember horn (plain grooved bands, hide stopper, a thread of smoke); the flicked coal.
- **Background:** A basalt terrace high above the Cradle (the green valley inside a ring of smoking mountains), steam rising from the valley.
- **Holo interaction:** Prism: the sun disc behind her shoulder near centre is the centred glow; keep the coal bed textured so it does not blow out.
- **Rarity ambition:** Ultra rare box-art: the whole card in one frame, the fire's blow taken, the stride unbroken, the coal already flying.

- **Card facts:** {2}{W}{W} · W · 2/5 · sentinel · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Sefa, keeper of the First Fire, the hearth the Hearth clan says was lit from the sun on its first morning. A Human Firekeeper, so no species tells. Her Provoked makes a Hatchling, so her secondary canon element is one newborn of the canon design (section 7), just hatched in the warm ash at the fire's edge.

- **Pose & composition:** Seen from a few steps back, head to knees, front three-quarter at the right-centre, standing upright and alert. The First Fire burns low in a ring of basalt stones at the centre-left, its bright heart at about y 470; she turns a glowing log with a long antler fire-fork held low, the fork lower than her shoulders, the flames never above her waist. - **Props / weapon:** The antler fire-fork; the ring hearth; the one Hatchling and its shell.
- **Background:** The wide mouth of a high sandstone cave over the Long Grass: the Cradle's green valley far below, steam off the fern forest, the ring of smoking mountains on the horizon.

### Oru, the Tyrant Queen — `fd-oru-tyrant-queen`
- **Card facts:** {4}{R}{G} · R/G (gold frame) · 5/5 · overrun · ur, legendary · holo: prism
- **Character & source:** Oru, a Dinokin Tyrant and the rung 28 Darling (R/G), the final tower portrait. The split lord: her Dinosaurs are Dreaded, so a plain tyrant looms at her shoulder. Thirties, tall and powerful, bronze skin, long dark-red hair. Tyrant tells: one massive tail, short brow ridges, serrated fangs; scaled patches are Dinokin skin, not a tell.
- **Personality / mood:** "Tyrant Queen" and Overrun: the valley goes where she walks.

### Woolly Rhinokin Guard — `fd-woolly-rhinokin`
- **Card facts:** {3}{W} · W · 3/4 · sentinel · r · holo: sheen
- **Character & source:** A Beastkin Rhino (D3: a megafauna girl, furred, never scaled), the Woolly Rhinokin of `beastkin.md`'s species sheet, keeping the Hearth clan's cold-end camp. Three tells, stated and no more: small furred side ears, two ivory nose-horns (one behind the other, the front one longer), and a shaggy russet-brown wool coat grown over her shoulders and upper back. The sheet's tufted tail is left out to keep the §4d cap of three (flagged). Thirties, massive and muscular, warm brown skin, dark brown hair in a thick low braid.
- **Personality / mood:** Sentinel and a Provoked that heals and hardens: hit her and she gets bigger, warmer and harder to shift. Stubborn good cheer.
- **Pose & composition:** Provoked: the blow survived. - **Character & source:** A Beastkin Sabretooth, the Tar clan's cave cats (brief section 2), with the three tells of the beastkin.md sheet's First Dawn row: short rounded cat ears, two long sabre fangs from the upper jaw past the lower lip, one short bobbed tail. Ears and fangs sit in the window. Twenties, athletic and long-limbed, tawny-brown skin, short tawny hair. Beastkin, not Dinokin: fur, never scales or feathers.
- **Personality / mood:** Nine Lives and Untouchable: nothing lands on her twice, and the first time did not take.
- **Pose & composition:** Nine Lives, the second landing: rising from one knee on a flat basalt boulder above the tar, weight coming forward, ash and grit still falling, one hand flat on the stone. The trace of where she went down: a scuffed hollow in the ash on the boulder behind her. Untouchable: one rival's thrown flint spear lies spent in the ash a pace off, missed. Exactly one cyan bead on her left upper arm (the Mark she came back with). Her head top, ears included, at or below y 179; bobbed tail at her hip, root and tip in frame.

- **Expression:** Amused, eyes half-lidded, fangs over a closed lower lip.
- **Props / weapon:** None of her own (the fangs are the Deathblade); the spent spear.
- **Background:** The Tar Flats, a basalt boulder field above the pools.

### Grave-Mourner — `fdc-grave-mourner-rite`
- **Card facts:** {2}{B} · B · 4/4 · c · holo: none
- **Character & source:** A Tar-clan Human Witch: Rite 1 (the payment, one of her own given up) and a mourner whose every loss costs the opponent. Forties, broad and sturdy, warm brown skin, black hair loose and streaked with ash.
- **Personality / mood:** Grief that sends the bill.
- **Pose & composition:** Rite, the payment and never the killing: kneeling on one knee at the tar's edge, front three-quarter, both hands laying one fallen hunter's flint spear flat onto the glossy surface, where it has begun to sink. No second figure. Her head top at or below y 179.
- **Costume & attire:** Tar-glossed hide two-piece, chest and hips covered, sinew ties, a long ash-grey hide mourning wrap around her shoulders, hood down.
- **Palette:** B `#5a3a70` / `#140d1c`, accent `#9b6fc4`; ash grey `#5b6166`; tar brown `#3d2a1a` / `#8a6a48`.
- **Lighting:** Low sun from the right, dawn-peach key on her face and the spear shaft; sky-blue rim on the wrap.
- **Expression:** Dry-eyed, jaw set, mouth grim at the corners.
- **Props / weapon:** One flint-headed spear with a bone-bead binding.
- **Background:** A still tar pool, steam, two values.
- **Holo interaction:** None: common.
- **Rarity ambition:** Common: one idea (the offering), readable at 119×97.

The four set tokens, in the section 7 order of index.md. Each is its canon design, shared by every entry that mints or foreshadows it; all four are beast-alone and open their Prompt with "NO woman".

### Hatchling — `tok-hatchling`
